# Agents · E · part 10

1,054 entries, evidence-mapper to execution-contract. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **evidence-mapper**: Build a reverse index mapping citations to the DAG edges, EPC events, and ODE parameters they support in Part V. Use when asking "which papers support this model component" or when checking for under-cited model elements. ([yannickloth/health-me-cfs](https://github.com/yannickloth/health-me-cfs/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/evidence-mapper.md))
- **evidence-matrix-admissibility**: Analyzes evidence for relevance, admissibility, authentication, hearsay, foundation, chain of custody, and strategic value. Use when building or updating an evidence matrix, planning exhibits and sponsoring witnesses, anticipating objections, or deci ([allenbenj/pages-main](https://github.com/allenbenj/pages-main/blob/HEAD/.kilo/agents/evidence-matrix-admissibility.agent.md))
- **evidence-matrix-reviewer**: 각 주장과 증거를 대조하여 갑질 인정 가능성, 보완 조사 필요성, 반대 가능성을 검토한다. ([cic8822-cpu/26.9.10.](https://github.com/cic8822-cpu/26.9.10./blob/HEAD/2.%EC%8B%A4%EC%8A%B5-%EB%AF%BC%EC%9B%90%EC%A1%B0%EC%82%AC%EB%B3%B4%EA%B3%A0%EC%84%9C/.claude/agents/evidence-matrix-reviewer.md))
- **evidence-miner**: Mines a period's git history, merged pull requests, reviews given, and the plugin's own redacted session digests across every repository, then returns candidate accomplishments clustered by the problem they solved. Spawned by /accomplishments:sweep when the period is large enough that raw mining output would flood the conversation. Read-only; proposes candidates rather than writing entries. ([bobtat/claude-plugins](https://github.com/bobtat/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/accomplishments/agents/evidence-miner.md) · ★2)
- **evidence-miner**: Background worker that processes raw dumps in product/inbox/ into evidence-ledger entries and routed feature requests. Invoked by pm-os skills (weekly, synthesize, triage); never proactively, never for code. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-miner.md) · ★1)
- **evidence-officer**: Owns the client ledger, re-cuts, attribution and case notes, and is the only writer of section 8 of PRACTICE.md. Reads the whole client record and the client's own history, plus sections 7 and 8, and never the rate card. Produces the re-cut rows, the attribution split and the evidence note on disk, never publishes, and hands the section 7 change back rather than writing it. Use once a client has a recorded baseline and enough time has passed to re-cut it, and for any claim from any stage that is a candidate for section 8. ([alishansnsn/retainer](https://github.com/alishansnsn/retainer/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-officer.md))
- **evidence-officer**: Use this agent to capture and seal evidence. Receives any URL or content artifact, archives it (Wayback + archive.today), computes SHA-256, optionally captures WARC, assigns an EV-NNNN identifier, and writes to the case evidence ledger. Every external fetch should pass through this agent. Pattern derived from huntkit's case lifecycle and UAP_OSINT primary-source schema. ([drtamar/The-hunter](https://github.com/drtamar/The-hunter/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-officer.md))
- **evidence-only**: Say only what the evidence shows. A quick integration or an overstated claim is not traction. ([twzrd-sol/twzrd-trust](https://github.com/twzrd-sol/twzrd-trust/blob/HEAD/.grok/agents/07-evidence-only.md))
- **evidence-packager**: Use this agent when an MSP needs to gather and assemble compliance evidence for a client against a named framework or control set, producing a source-cited package an auditor or client can review. Trigger for: compliance evidence, gather evidence for audit, build evidence package, SOC2 evidence, HIPAA evidence, CIS evidence, audit prep, evidence for auditor, control evidence package. Examples: "Build a SOC 2 evidence package for Meridian Health", "Gather HIPAA evidence for Riverside Medical before Friday's audit", "Put together CIS control evidence across the whole portfolio" ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/compliance-pack/agents/evidence-packager.md) · ★55)
- **evidence-processor**: Catalog and process newly collected evidence — generate missing .meta.md files, compute SHA-256 hashes, extract entities, flag duplicates. Use after any batch of evidence lands in evidence/, or when the evidence inventory needs an audit. ([lovesinha69/osint-investigator](https://github.com/lovesinha69/osint-investigator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-processor.md))
- **evidence-prose-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks evidence atoms, writer-visible facts, visible notes, and claim-safety leakage risks. ([prashanthkrishnan91/claude_travelapp_pk91](https://github.com/prashanthkrishnan91/claude_travelapp_pk91/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-prose-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **evidence-provenance-agent**: Tracks the source, licence, and confidence of every external component, snippet, and claim so nothing unverified or unlicensed enters the project unmarked. ([Suraj787/motif](https://github.com/Suraj787/motif/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-provenance-agent.md))
- **evidence-quality-review**: Reviews document deliverables for claim support, source quality, and factual accuracy. Invoked by /acd:review during non-software sessions. ([DEVEDANOS/acd-plugin](https://github.com/DEVEDANOS/acd-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-quality-review.md))
- **evidence-rationale-reviewer**: Reviews material claims, decisions, assumptions, alternatives, evidence strength and residual risk before publication. Never changes the underlying decision. ([leogalambos/JAI](https://github.com/leogalambos/JAI/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/evidence-rationale-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **evidence-reader**: Read an existing cortex-epd report, trace its findings to material evidence and explain unresolved or corrective-action items without rerunning or changing the review. ([HiQ-AI/plugins](https://github.com/HiQ-AI/plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/cortex-epd/agents/evidence-reader.md))
- **evidence-reader**: Deep-reads everything one account posted in the window and returns structured evidence notes. One dispatch per account. ([rabindrakharel/invest](https://github.com/rabindrakharel/invest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-reader.md))
- **evidence-recorder**: Methodology: CRUD-R-A Profile B (fallback EVIDENCE recorder when no kind-specialist fits). EN: Fallback Profile B agent for phases that produce evidence but don't fit a specialist recorder (code-reviewer / tester / security-expert / architect-reviewer / system-dev). Takes whatever raw input the orchestrator hands in — a log file path, a benchmark output, a manual QA result, a deployment validation note, a UX session transcript, an external audit report — plus a verdict directive, and structures it into a canonical forgeplan EVIDENCE artifact. Records what was observed, not what should be true… ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/agents-pro/agents/evidence-recorder.md) · ★4)
- **evidence-release-steward**: &gt; Evidence and Release Steward — evidence ledger, DoD only after PASS, draft PR, never auto-merge. ([tjsasakifln/extra-cli](https://github.com/tjsasakifln/extra-cli/blob/HEAD/squads/extra-dod-roi/agents/evidence-release-steward.md) · ★1)
- **evidence-reporter**: PAIASA DEV evidence reporter. Used in the production profile. Assembles the verifiable evidence package for a finished task (AC-to-test mapping, test and build results, review outcomes, scans, screenshots, staging link, deviations, open items) for the human reviewers and the PAIASA PM team's acceptance check. ([harymedire/paiasa-dev](https://github.com/harymedire/paiasa-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-reporter.md))
- **evidence-reporter**: Packages build, review, architecture, and runtime evidence without altering implementation. ([mazinmarji/nornyx-forge-live-demo](https://github.com/mazinmarji/nornyx-forge-live-demo/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-reporter.md))
- **evidence-researcher**: Web research sub-agent with citation discipline, for the Level 0 evidence bar and any claim that needs external grounding. Gathers independent signals across multiple channels, attaches a source and a confidence to every claim, and runs in an isolated context so the research does not pollute the main session. Returns structured findings, not prose. ([Capx-AI/casa](https://github.com/Capx-AI/casa/blob/HEAD/agents/evidence-researcher.md) · ★51)
- **evidence-researcher**: Investigate bounded PC 03040008 / PS4 02020005 function, type, resource-layout, packet and provenance questions with read-only IDA inspection tools and the repository's dumps. ([ddon-research/ddon-data](https://github.com/ddon-research/ddon-data/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-researcher.md) · ★7)
- **evidence-researcher**: Gathers external evidence from competitors, public workflows, job postings, and reviews ([AustinDKB/verse-cue](https://github.com/AustinDKB/verse-cue/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/evidence-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **evidence-researcher**: Leaf evidence worker for one controller-assigned material uncertainty requiring scoped repository facts or current official or primary public sources. When the prompt hook selects focused evidence research, execute this one route before material generation or recommendation; otherwise use only with a bounded controller-assigned question. - **evidence-researcher**: Use this agent when you need to search for information, verify facts, research topics, validate claims, or prepare materials for reports, presentations, or news briefings. - **evidence-researcher**: Council member. Investigates one assigned question and reports what the evidence actually supports, separating what it verified from what it inferred. - **evidence-researcher**: 仅在需要派发核验子代理时读取。使用平台现有代理能力，不假设本文件会自动注册一个可执行代理类型。 ([phoben/agent-skills](https://github.com/phoben/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/.trellis/tasks/11-04-issue-hub-product/artifacts/account-menu-final/install-033/plugins/yg-toolkit/skills/issue-extract/agents/evidence-researcher.md) · 9 copies)

- **evidence-resolver**: Use to gather current evidence for a specific freshness-sensitive question and return it as a structured EvidenceReceipt. Resolves package versions from npm/PyPI, checks HTTP freshness headers, and fetches official documentation. Returns receipts with TTLs, never a fabricated version on failure. - **evidence-retention**: Use this lifecycle for development and release evidence. Collect only what the run needs to prove its claims and resume work. Set `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1` before launching Python. Do not copy a whole sandbox, project, or home directory into evidence. Keep generated caches and disposable fixture state out of durable evidence. Record a tested binary or archive's identity, commands, results, check ([yersonargotev/packy](https://github.com/yersonargotev/packy/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/evidence-retention.md) · ★1)
- **evidence-retriever**: Read-only retrieval subagent. Given a scoped query, fetches web and local material, then returns a synthesised summary with citations. Used by `/desk-research` standard and deep modes and by `/compare-hypotheses` for per-hypothesis parallel retrieval. - **evidence-review**: What a reviewer checks on any PR that carries acceptance evidence (a live-host run, a probe log, a release record). Executes ONE described UI flow via playwright tools, captures screenshots and console/network evidence, returns distilled step-by-step evidence. Spawned by /execute or the executor agent — not for direct human use. ([AlexanderBorysenko/alex-makoto-plugins](https://github.com/AlexanderBorysenko/alex-makoto-plugins/blob/HEAD/project-executor/agents/exec-browser.md))
- **exec-code**: Executant d'un lot CODE confie par /dispatch (implementation bornee, tests, refactor local). Spec claire, aucun choix d'architecture. ([Grozef/claude-config](https://github.com/Grozef/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-code.md))
- **exec-comms-writer**: Use for short executive communications — Slack announcements, all-hands one-pagers, internal email blasts, leadership email replies, exec social posts. Short-form, voice-of-the-leader. For long-form vision pieces use vision-memo-writer. ([lswank/black-book](https://github.com/lswank/black-book/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-comms-writer.md) · ★1)
- **exec-compile-specialist**: Compiles TensorRT-LLM from source. Handles two scenarios: (1) compiling directly on a compute node inside a dev Docker container, and (2) submitting a SLURM batch job from a login node with enroot container support. Detects the environment automatically, gathers required parameters, runs the build, monitors progress, and verifies success. ([NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exec-compile-specialist.md) · ★15k · 3 copies)
- **exec-critical**: FRONTIER-DO tier at high effort. Execution agent for correctness-critical implementation in an isolated git worktree. Writes code, tests, and commits on its own branch. Use where a silent wrong answer is expensive - concurrency, locking, derivation logic, foundations other phases build on. Not for mechanical edits (use exec-mechanical) and not for the single subtlest workstream of a plan (use exec-subtle). ([LCS-Adam/standing-orders](https://github.com/LCS-Adam/standing-orders/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-critical.md) · ★1)
- **exec-experiments**: Experiment execution lab (sonnet). Designs and runs the project's experiments, stress tests, and fair comparisons; saves result tables. Read-only on the deliverable (executes); writes a note + CSVs. ([pkjaslam/Cambium_AI](https://github.com/pkjaslam/Cambium_AI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/11-exec-experiments.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **exec-gate**: Runs one stage-4 entry's gate commands once, as given, and reports exactly what they printed - each red quoted and tagged code or machine, a red test outside the diff run once more, the surface the diff touches, and the stack brought up for the QAs when green. Writes no code and judges nothing. Dispatched by exec-entry-workflow.js. ([farias-77/skills](https://github.com/farias-77/skills/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/exec-gate.md) · ★3)
- **exec-gemi**: FlowMic EXEC-GEMI（gemini-3.8-flash-high）——九语文案写作与母语复核、按主控写好的提示词执行的文案类任务；只写派卡指定的结果文件；TIER: simple ([flowmicapp/flowmic](https://github.com/flowmicapp/flowmic/blob/HEAD/.devin/agents/exec-gemi.md) · ★2)
- **exec-generic**: Builds one plan slice — writes the captured patch a plan.md slice describes, inside the worktree path given literally in the task. ([ayusavin/skills](https://github.com/ayusavin/skills/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/exec-generic.md) · ★3)
- **exec-haiku-high**: Executor for tricky but well-specified single-module logic: parsing, validation, edge-case-heavy functions; also the start tier of every Sonnet-planned task. Receives a plan path, a task ID and a Skills line (at most 4 skills); works test-first and commits on its task branch, never pushes or merges. ([Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-haiku-high.md) · ★1)
- **exec-haiku-low**: Executor for renames, config and environment changes, boilerplate following existing patterns, adding fields, copy and docs changes, and tests from cases given in the plan. Receives a plan path, a task ID and a Skills line (at most 4 skills); works test-first and commits on its task branch, never pushes or merges. ([Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-haiku-low.md) · ★1)
- **exec-haiku-med**: Executor for single-module logic with clear tests: serializers, simple endpoints, utilities, small components. Receives a plan path, a task ID and a Skills line (at most 4 skills); works test-first and commits on its task branch, never pushes or merges. ([Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-haiku-med.md) · ★1)
- **exec-high**: Исполнитель задачи доски, когда agentctl pick выдал effort high (неопределённость 3: в деталях есть развилки; сюда же неполные метаданные строки и любой вердикт уровня base, ему маппинг ниже high не выдаёт). Модель диспетчер передаёт параметром по первой строке вердикта. ([dronrider/devkit](https://github.com/dronrider/devkit/blob/HEAD/kit/agents/exec-high.md))
- **exec-instrumenter**: Mechanical debug-log injector/stripper for project-executor. Inserts tagged EXEC-TRACE log lines at caller-specified locations, maintains the instrumentation registry, and strips tags idempotently with git-diff verification. Spawned by /execute — not for direct human use. ([AlexanderBorysenko/alex-makoto-plugins](https://github.com/AlexanderBorysenko/alex-makoto-plugins/blob/HEAD/project-executor/agents/exec-instrumenter.md))
- **exec-iris-test**: 🌈 Runs the quality gate — lint, types, tests, build, coverage and acceptance, plus a three-mode visual review against spec, production parity and mobile UX — and returns a structured pass/fail verdict. Use when work is written and someone must decide whether it holds. Not for writing the fix; it grades, it does not author. ([ignromanov/conclave](https://github.com/ignromanov/conclave/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-iris-test.md))
- **exec-iteration**: Experiment-iteration loop owner. Runs an experiment, diagnoses the result, tunes, and re-runs — a budget-aware diagnose→improve→re-run loop with branch/prune (tree-search) exploration. Replaces single-shot execution. Runs code. ([pkjaslam/Cambium_AI](https://github.com/pkjaslam/Cambium_AI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/35-exec-iteration.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **exec-lane**: Conductor's default execution lane since 2026-09-22. Use for well-specified implementation, refactors, migrations, terminal/CI/infra work, and test authoring. Pinned to Opus 5.5 at high effort (Ivan, 19 Oct 2026: implementation runs at high, not medium). Use design-lane for taste-critical surfaces, bulk-lane for mechanical sweeps, and an Agent or Workflow lane with effort medium for volume writing. ([IvanOboth/conductor-plugin](https://github.com/IvanOboth/conductor-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-lane.md) · ★1)
- **exec-local-slurm**: Execute a TensorRT-LLM workload on a local Slurm cluster. Supports persistent allocation (allocate once via nohup salloc, reuse across runs) and one-shot sbatch. Workflow-agnostic — handles pytest, eval, benchmark, and custom scripts identically. The orchestrator (typically trtllm-case-executor) writes a job spec to &lt;work_dir&gt;/job_spec.json and invokes this agent to run it. ([NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exec-local-slurm.md) · ★15k · 2 copies)
- **exec-long-running-root-cause**: The five root causes behind the long-running-exec failure mode, the verified client-timeout table for the agents Ralph owns today, and the seam each cause maps to. The child runtime shutdown path lands at `ralph/mcp/server/runtime.py` (via `exit_when_owner_exits` and standalone server termination); the page you are reading records what the causes ARE so a future agent can tell a fix that addressed ([Ralph-Workflow/Ralph-Workflow](https://github.com/Ralph-Workflow/Ralph-Workflow/blob/HEAD/ralph-workflow/docs/agents/exec-long-running-root-cause.md) · ★6)
- **exec-low**: Исполнитель задачи доски, когда agentctl pick выдал effort low (неопределённость 0: рутина, размышлять не над чем). Модель диспетчер передаёт параметром по первой строке вердикта. ([dronrider/devkit](https://github.com/dronrider/devkit/blob/HEAD/kit/agents/exec-low.md))
- **Exec-Manager**: Owns the full lifecycle of a single implementation plan. Spawns Exec-Executor (per phase), QA-Reviewer (after completion), and Exec-Fixer (on review issues). Handles fix cycles internally — only escalates true blockers. Invokable directly for single-plan execution or via Director for multi-plan features. ([xiaden/nomarr](https://github.com/xiaden/nomarr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/exec-manager.agent.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **exec-mechanical**: SMALL tier at medium effort. Execution agent for mechanical, well-specified edits in an isolated git worktree - JSON/config entry removal and addition, plist keys, verified-safe deletions. Commits on its own branch. Use only where the change is fully specified in advance and a test or an empirical check proves it safe. Anything requiring judgment goes to a larger tier. ([LCS-Adam/standing-orders](https://github.com/LCS-Adam/standing-orders/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-mechanical.md) · ★1)
- **exec-medium**: Исполнитель задачи доски, когда agentctl pick выдал effort medium (неопределённость 1-2: подход выбран, открыты детали). Модель диспетчер передаёт параметром по первой строке вердикта. ([dronrider/devkit](https://github.com/dronrider/devkit/blob/HEAD/kit/agents/exec-medium.md))
- **exec-metron-rank**: 📐 Ranks competing candidates — takes several attempts at the same task and orders them by merit so the weakest are dropped before expensive review. Use when more than one candidate exists and something must choose between them. Not for producing candidates, issuing a pass/fail verdict, or a single-candidate run, where ranking is a no-op. ([ignromanov/conclave](https://github.com/ignromanov/conclave/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-metron-rank.md))
- **exec-opus**: Implements exactly one planned subtask flagged hard by the Nightshift planner, in its own worktree. Makes no design decisions. ([jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade](https://github.com/jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-opus.md))
- **exec-opus**: ../../agents/exec-opus.md ([jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade](https://github.com/jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exec-opus.md))
- **exec-preview**: Critic that previews how an executive will grill a strategy or problem-statement doc - cost, value, data, what we stop doing, and what happens if a bet is wrong. Use before an executive review or road show. Pass it the path of the doc to read. ([sbehrens/qcon-strategy-plugin](https://github.com/sbehrens/qcon-strategy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/strategy-playbook/agents/exec-preview.md) · ★9)
- **exec-remote-slurm**: Execute a TensorRT-LLM workload on a remote Slurm cluster via SSH. Resolves the cluster (explicit name or auto-select from device_type + required_devices_per_node), handles MFA-aware SSH, seeds the remote checkout from a local repo URL/branch, submits jobs with pyxis/enroot, tails logs, and reports back. The orchestrator (typically trtllm-case-executor) writes a job spec to &lt;work_dir&gt;/job_spec.json and invokes this agent to run it. ([NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exec-remote-slurm.md) · ★15k · 2 copies)
- **exec-report-generator**: Transform the security-assessment pipeline's structured artifacts into a publication-ready report suitable for CISO / CTO distribution. Match the four-document output shape of the `opus_repo_scan_test` reference (per-repo × N + cross-repo summary) with presentational severity that business readers understand. ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-assessment/docs/agents/exec-report-generator.md) · ★309)
- **exec-report-generator**: Synthesizes the publication-ready executive report from upstream artifacts. Emits a 7-section per-repo report (plus cross-repo summary for multi-repo runs). ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-assessment/agents/exec-report-generator.md) · ★309)
- **exec-reviewer**: C-level executive gate reviewer for strategic planning. Lens-injected at spawn time as CTO, CMO, or COO. Reviews specs and panel scorecards to make Go/Conditional Go/Recycle/Kill decisions from their executive perspective. Uses Opus for high-quality strategic reasoning. ([danieliser/strategic-planner](https://github.com/danieliser/strategic-planner/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **exec-reviewer**: Reviews a document the way a sharp, time-poor VP or C-level executive would — the first 90 seconds first, then the full read — flagging buried leads, unsupported numbers, missing asks, and the questions the PM will get ambushed with in the meeting. Use when the user says "review this before I send it up", "how will leadership read this", "poke holes in my memo/deck", or is preparing any upward-facing document. &lt;example&gt; user: "I'm sending this strategy memo to our CPO tomorrow — review it first?" assistant: I'll run the exec-reviewer agent on it: it reads the memo the way your CPO will — 90 s… ([ramanbamba/10x-pm](https://github.com/ramanbamba/10x-pm/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **exec-reviewer**: Reviews a PRD as a busy executive would. Use from spec-review to test strategic fit, clarity of the ask, the business case and whether the success metrics matter. ([Mosin112/pm-harness](https://github.com/Mosin112/pm-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-reviewer.md))
- **exec-reviewer**: Reviews a PRD, decision doc or update the way a busy senior leader would - looking for the ask, the evidence and the business impact in the first minute. Use before sending anything to a VP, GM or exec review. ([rahulmehta888/PMOS](https://github.com/rahulmehta888/PMOS/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-reviewer.md))
- **exec-runner**: Mechanical command executor for project-executor. Runs ONE command (start/stop/test/build/seed/probe) with a readiness check, captures output to files, and returns distilled evidence only. Spawned by /execute or the executor agent — not for direct human use. ([AlexanderBorysenko/alex-makoto-plugins](https://github.com/AlexanderBorysenko/alex-makoto-plugins/blob/HEAD/project-executor/agents/exec-runner.md))
- **exec-scout-research**: 🔭 Gathers evidence before a decision — searches the codebase, the web and the wiki, corrects the scope of the question, and returns ranked options with citations. Read-only. Use when an advisor needs grounded facts rather than a guess. Not for writing code, issuing verdicts, or making the decision itself. ([ignromanov/conclave](https://github.com/ignromanov/conclave/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-scout-research.md))
- **exec-seat**: Exec room seat (CEO, CPO or CMO, named in the brief) for audience-facing milestones before the spec. Writes one short position paper with options for the owner. Read-only except the paper file. ([alchristleo/lean-sdd](https://github.com/alchristleo/lean-sdd/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-seat.md) · 2 copies)
- **exec-simple**: Executant d'un lot MECANIQUE confie par /dispatch (le brief contient le code ou le texte exact a ecrire, ou correction mecanique dans un seul fichier). Transcription et verification, aucune decision a prendre. ([Grozef/claude-config](https://github.com/Grozef/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-simple.md))
- **exec-socra-critic**: 🔍 Attacks a proposal to find what is wrong with it — runs five red-team techniques against a candidate and records the refutations for the judge to weigh. Read-only. Use when a decision looks sound and someone should try to break it first. Not for producing the work, fixing what it finds, or pronouncing the final verdict. ([ignromanov/conclave](https://github.com/ignromanov/conclave/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-socra-critic.md))
- **exec-sonnet**: ../../agents/exec-sonnet.md ([jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade](https://github.com/jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exec-sonnet.md))
- **exec-sonnet**: Implements exactly one planned subtask from a Nightshift plan, in its own worktree. Makes no design decisions. ([jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade](https://github.com/jacksongeiger/Claude_Upgrade/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-sonnet.md))
- **exec-sonnet-high**: Executor for cross-module changes, refactors, Celery and BullMQ work, Docker and terminal-heavy run-fix loops, and non-trivial merge conflicts. Receives a plan path, a task ID and a Skills line; works test-first and commits on its task branch, never pushes or merges. ([Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-sonnet-high.md) · ★1)
- **exec-sonnet-med**: Executor for standard multi-file feature work across roughly two to five files. Receives a plan path, a task ID and a Skills line; works test-first and commits on its task branch, never pushes or merges. ([Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-sonnet-med.md) · ★1)
- **exec-sonnet-xhigh**: Delegation ceiling executor: only for tasks with three yes answers in tier selection, or after a failure at exec-sonnet-high. Receives a plan path, a task ID and a Skills line; works test-first and commits on its task branch, never pushes or merges. ([Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/Alirezajalilii/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-sonnet-xhigh.md) · ★1)
- **exec-sponsor**: Provides executive oversight on strategic renewals, unblockers for escalations, and alignment with corporate objectives. ([gtmagents/gtm-agents](https://github.com/gtmagents/gtm-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/renewal-orchestration/agents/exec-sponsor.md) · ★447 · 5 copies)
- **exec-standard**: MID tier at medium effort. Execution agent for well-bounded implementation in an isolated git worktree - new scripts against a proven local pattern, wording changes to an agent definition, signal-only tooling, smoke verification. Writes code, tests, and commits on its own branch. Not for concurrency or derivation logic (use exec-critical or exec-subtle). ([LCS-Adam/standing-orders](https://github.com/LCS-Adam/standing-orders/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-standard.md) · ★1)
- **exec-subtle**: FRONTIER-DO tier at max effort. ULTRATHINK execution agent for the single subtlest workstream in a plan - derivation changes with mixed historical data, flags that must resist an existing self-heal, invariants with a crying-wolf failure mode. Writes code, tests, and commits in an isolated worktree. Reserve it; use exec-critical for ordinary correctness-critical work. ([LCS-Adam/standing-orders](https://github.com/LCS-Adam/standing-orders/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-subtle.md) · ★1)
- **exec-summary-agent**: Synthesizes the finished Sections 2-13 into the SCQA Executive Summary (Section 1). Gated — must not run until the Orchestrator confirms all 13 other sections are drafted. ([sherifkaroub83-cell/talabat-retention-agentic-os](https://github.com/sherifkaroub83-cell/talabat-retention-agentic-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exec-summary-agent.md))
- **exec-supervisor**: Coordinates the execution and verification phase with checkpoint rollback and cleanup orchestration. ([Konstantilieris/Bugfow_Plugin](https://github.com/Konstantilieris/Bugfow_Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-supervisor.md) · ★1)
- **exec-techne-skills**: 🧰 Gets the capability a task needs into the agent that will do it — searches the skill registry, installed plugins and the engine's own tree, verifies what it finds, installs from allow-listed sources, and binds the result into the agent's definition rather than suggesting it. Use when a task needs know-how the assigned agent does not already carry. Not for deciding what the task is, judging the work, or installing from a source the operator has not allowed. ([ignromanov/conclave](https://github.com/ignromanov/conclave/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-techne-skills.md))
- **exec-themis-judge**: ⚖️ Issues the binding verdict — weighs the measured results against the critic's refutations and rules, with citations and a stated confidence. Use when evidence is in and someone must decide, across domains, whether the work stands. Not for producing or repairing the work; it only evaluates. ([ignromanov/conclave](https://github.com/ignromanov/conclave/blob/HEAD/agents/exec-themis-judge.md))
- **exec-update**: Prepares exec-facing updates (steering group, monthly programme update, leadership email) that read in 90 seconds. - **executable-knowledge**: Markdown is the durable truth. IWE supplies semantic structure. marimo supplies executable structure. marimohub supplies the runtime.
